利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本

固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

### Page 3

Largan Precision (3008.TW)
08 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Seeing multiple generation and years opportunities — Compared with these
broader solutions providers, Largan remains primarily as a high-precision
manufacturing and assembly specialist. Management described its current
business relationship as closer to contract manufacturing, with direct discussions
with end customers focused mainly on next-generation products. On product
architecture, the initial 2027 commercial project remains focused on single-row FA

using grating coupling (GC), while multi-row and multilayer configurations remain in
sampling and are primarily associated with 2028 programs. Management believes
its FA/FAU solution is broadly applicable to both CPO and NPO when the underlying
optical interface remains similar, because the primary difference is the placement of
the optical package. Largan’s primary differentiation is not the FA design itself but
its ability to manufacture highly precise assembly using imperfect input

components.
Automation and Yield Control Are the Real Competitive
Advantages
During the earnings call, Largan’s management emphasized that almost all critical
FA/FAU alignment equipment is developed internally, including machinery and
control software. This enables faster equipment modification, process
optimization and troubleshooting compared with labor-intensive assembly.
Automated production also offers better traceability and root-cause analysis when

yield problems emerge. Management stated that customer feedback is positive
and the FAU certification process progressing well. The key competitive advantage,
in our view, is the ability to combine submicron accuracy, high throughput and
reproducible yields at scale, rather than precision alone.
In our view, the earnings call supports a more constructive medium-term view of
Largan’s CPO opportunities than our previous assessment, particularly given the

apparent completion of initial FA qualification, customer interest in integrated FAU
assembly and a clearer path toward commercial production in 2H27. Moving from
standalone FA to FAU and potentially carrier assembly could materially increase
revenue content per optical connection, while the company’s automated
manufacturing capabilities may support differentiation in increasingly complex,
high-density optical architectures. The next growth opportunity could emerge from

multi-row FA and higher-channel-count optical solutions beginning in 2028,
particularly as AI networking bandwidth moves beyond current-generation
platforms.
Maintaining Buy on better FAU visibility
Given the weak smartphone order flow and GM pressure in the near term, we revise
down our net income projections by 9%/29% for 2026/2027, but slightly raise our
2028 net income projection by 1% given the solid growth opportunity in FAU
business. However, we think Largan is transitioning from a smartphone optics

manufacturer into a potential high-precision optical interconnect assembly
supplier. As they are moving to FAU not just FA; we assume higher revenue
contribution for 2027 /2028 of 28%/ 44% considering Largan could get 15%/30%
market share.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Largan Precision (3008.TW)
08 October 2026 Citi Research
8
Largan Precision
Company description
Largan is a plastic/glass/hybrid lens supplier in Taiwan with end-applications
including phone cameras, DSCs, MFPs, and tablets. Its new development into
FAU business should server as a long term growth opportunity.
Investment strategy
We rate Largan a Buy as we expect its core iPhone business to remain resilient
and its co-packaged optics (CPO) development to provide strong upside

potential in the longer term. We believe Largan could be a long-term winner in
the lens market driven by: 1) its technology leadership; 2) intellectual property
(IP) protection; and 3) higher yield vs. competitors. In the longer term, we
believe spec migration could keep Largan ahead of competitors.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$8,500 for Largan shares is based on 33x 2027 /28E
EPS. We are using 2027 /28E EPS average as we see new Fiber Array + PMLA

revenue to kick in from next year. Our 33x PER is higher than its historical 5-
year PER range, which we think is justified by its better order visibility proven
by its recent strong capacity expansion plan, and we expect its CPO business
engagement should provide a strong long-term growth opportunity.
Risks
Key downside risks include: 1) weaker smartphone demand or replacement
demand, and in particular, demand headwinds for certain key clients; 2)

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
September  Sales +5% MoM/
+14% YoY
In this report, we focus on E Ink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• September sales were NT$3,613mn (+5% MoM/+14% YoY).
• 3Q26 sales came in at NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), in line with our
estimate at NT$9,985mn (-2% QoQ/-4% YoY) and the consensus at NT
$9,774mn (-4% QoQ/-6% YoY).
Our view:
• E Ink's monthly sales run rate will need to stay at the September level

through year end to achieve its full-year revenue guidance of up 10-15% YoY
for 2026. This implies its 4Q26 revenue will be up by 9% QoQ, which still
looks within reach, despite the softer seasonality
• While the worst seems to be behind us and valuation is at a trough level of
12x 2027e P/E, we see no strong catalysts in the near term, despite our long-
term positive view on  e-paper demand across various applications. Stay EW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus
EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.

Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 188: 9月美国通胀数据点评：通胀再度升温，年内加息预期强化

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月美国通胀数据点评：通胀再度升温，年内加息预期强化.pdf
Report source: 爱建证券
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告
总量研究 / 宏观研究 2026 年 10 月 19 日
细分研究
通胀再度升温，年内加息预期强化
——9 月美国通胀数据点评
相关研究
-
投资要点：
 事件：10 月 14 日美国劳工部公布通胀数据显示，9 月美国 CPI 同比增速达 3.7%，环比增速
达 0.6%。
 核心观点：9 月美国通胀反弹，能源价格上涨是主要推动因素，核心通胀则延续温和修复走
势。（1）能源价格大幅上行推高整体 CPI，住房及部分服务价格仍对核心通胀形成支撑。（2）
通胀水平走高，抬升市场对美联储年内加息的预期；另一方面，近期就业市场边际走弱，限
制美联储进一步收紧货币政策的空间。市场预期美联储 10 月维持利率不变的概率较高，12
月加息 25BP 的概率较高。
 总体通胀：能源价格大幅上涨，推动 CPI 上行。
 9 月美国整体通胀明显回升，主要受能源价格上涨推动，食品价格相对平稳。（1）能源价格上
升是推动通胀上行的主要动力。9 月能源价格环比上涨 5.2%，其中汽油价格上涨 9.1%，对

整体 CPI 涨幅贡献超过一半。受能源价格影响，整体 CPI 同比增速由 3.4%升至 3.7%。结构
看，通胀回升更多体现为能源价格冲击，而非各类商品和服务价格的普遍上涨。（2）食品价
格整体平稳，对通胀的推动作用有限。食品类价格环比持平，其中家庭食品价格小幅下降，外
出就餐价格有所上涨。食品价格总体保持温和，尚未形成明显涨价压力。总体看，CPI 上行主
因在于能源价格上涨驱动，能源价格是否继续高位运行，将影响后续通胀整体走势。
 核心通胀：涨幅温和，服务价格仍具粘性。
 9 月美国核心通胀小幅上行，价格压力仍未消退。剔除食品和能源后的核心 CPI 环比上涨
0.3%，同比由 2.4%涨至 2.6%。结构看，不同领域价格表现分化。（1）住房价格延续温和上
涨。住房价格环比上涨 0.4%，较前月略上行。住房价格在核心 CPI 中占比较高，显示核心通
胀仍具备一定上行支撑。（2）部分商品和服务表现分化。新车、服装、交通服务等价格上行，
推动核心 CPI 维持正增长。二手车和卡车、医疗保健商品价格有所下降，显示核心通胀尚未

全面加速上行。整体而言，核心通胀仍处于温和上升区间，但住房和部分商品与服务价格粘
性，使通胀回落面临一定阻力。
 政策预期：通胀上行抬升加息概率，年内紧缩预期强化。
 通胀上行抬高市场对美联储加息的预期，就业市场走弱则限制加息力度。（1）通胀上升增强
年内加息必要性。整体 CPI 与核心 CPI 同比同步上行，通胀水平离美联储目标仍有差距。若
能源价格持续维持高位或上涨，并传导至核心商品和服务，美联储则继续维持偏紧的政策立
场，通过加息抑制通胀。（2）就业走弱限制加息力度。新增非农就业下行，失业率上行，劳动
市场走弱，降低美联储大幅加息的可能。通胀上行叠加就业走弱，市场预计美联储在 10 月议
息会议上仍保持利率水平不变，12 月大概率加息 25BP，年内加息概率强化。
 总结而言，9 月美国通胀上行主因在于能源价格上涨推动，核心通胀则延续温和走势。能源
价格大幅上行抬升整体通胀压力，住房及部分商品和服务价格对核心通胀形成支撑，通胀回
落仍面临阻力。通胀上行，美联储年内加息预期强化；同时，就业走弱制衡美联储大幅紧缩。

 风险提示：美国就业数据超预期，美国通胀水平超预期，地缘冲突风险。
证券分析师
陈国文

投资建议的评级标准
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为股票评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内的相对市场表现，也即：
以报告发布日后的 6 个月内的公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅作为基准。其中：A 股市场：沪深 300
指数（000300.SH）；新三板市场：三板成指（899001.CSI）（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（899002.CSI）（针对做市转让标的）；
北交所市场：北证 50 指数（899050. BJ）；香港市场：恒生指数（HIS.HI）；美国市场：标普 500 指数（SPX.GI）或纳斯达克指数（IXIC.GI）。
股票评级
买入 相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅大于 15%
增持 相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 5%～15%之间
持有 相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在-5%～5%之间

卖出 相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅小于-5%
行业评级
强于大市 相对表现优于同期相关证券市场代表性指数
中性 相对表现与同期相关证券市场代表性指数持平
弱于大市 相对表现弱于同期相关证券市场代表性指数

《上下游物价指标分歧加大》20261014

《前三季度进出口数据点评》20261014

9 月金融数据点评

关注外部环境对适度宽松货币政策的影响   
9 月新增社融和新增信贷均好于万得一致预期；企业融资需求分层，信用度
较高的企业以企业债为代表的融资需求下降，依赖贷款渠道的企业贷款 9 月
表现相对更好；居民部门加杠杆意愿不强；受资本市场波动影响，存款搬家
有变化的趋势；关注海外加息周期和高利率环境对国内金融条件和货币政策
的潜在影响。 
 9 月新增社融 3.55 万亿元，较去年同期多增 192 亿元，较 8 月多增 1.89 万亿元，
高于万得一致预期的 3.13 万亿元。9 月社融存量同比增长 7.1%，较 8 月下降了
0.07 个百分点，基本持平于万得一致预期的 7.06%。9 月社融口径新增人民币贷
款 1.27 万亿元，较去年同期少增 3342 亿元，较 8 月多增 1.22 万亿元。 
 9 月新增社融较去年同期小幅多增，主要原因是政府债券较去年同期多增了 3438
亿元，同时委托贷款和外币贷款也较去年同期有所多增；从当月新增规模看，新
增融资规模较大的项目，主要是政府债券、人民币贷款和汇票，当月少增的是信
托贷款和企业债。 值得关注的是9 月股票融资表现依然较好， 当月新增658 亿元，
较去年同期也有小幅上升， 但企业债少增425 亿元， 表明企业融资需求依然不强。 
 从社融存量结构来看，2026 年 9 月与 8 月相比，占比上升的有政府债券（上升
0.17 个百分点）和汇票（上升 0.04 个百分点），占比下降的有股票融资（下降
0.01 个百分点）、信托贷款（下降 0.02 个百分点）、企业债（下降 0.08 个百分
点）和人民币贷款（下降 0.16 个百分点）。与去年同期相比，9 月占比上升较多
的是政府债券（上升 1.30 个百分点）和企业债（上升 0.16 个百分点），占比下
降较多的是人民币贷款 （下降1.31 个百分点） 和委托贷款 （下降0.13 个百分点） 。
虽然新增企业债融资规模少增，但企业债融资占比仍有上升趋势。 
 9 月 M2 同比增长 7.2%，较 8 月下降 0.3 个百分点；M1 同比增长 3.2%，较 8 月
下降 0.9 个百分点；M0 同比增长 10.6%，较 8 月下降 0.6 个百分点。9 月活期存
款（M1-M0）环比上升 0.79%，定期存款（M2-M1）环比上升 0.76%，与去年同
期相比，活期存款环比增速偏低，定期存款环比增速偏高，关注资本市场指数波
动对存款变化趋势的影响。 
 9 月关注非银存款回流。9 月金融机构新增存款 1.77 万亿元，多增的主要是居民
存款 2.40 万亿元和企业存款 1.31 万亿元，财政存款和非银存款均少增；与去年
同期相比，9 月新增存款少增 4400 亿元，其中非银存款少增 8500 亿元，居民存
款少增 5600 亿元。9 月新增存款不弱， 但与去年同期相较不强， 其中值得关注的
细节较多：一是企业存款多增，一定程度上印证了企业融资需求不强的原因，二
是居民存款较去年同期少增，关注居民收入增速变化情况，三是财政存款较去年
同期多增， 关注财政支出是否偏慢， 以及对固投增速的影响， 四是非银存款偏弱，
可能代表投资者对资本市场的态度短期偏谨慎。 
 9 月实体经济融资需求延续偏弱。9 月金融机构口径新增人民币贷款 1.08 万亿元，
略高于万得一致预期的 9880 亿元。 其中， 新增短贷及票据3200 亿元， 中长贷9774
亿元， 企业贷1.29 万亿元。 我们认为有三处值得关注， 一是虽然新增贷款超预期，
但是较去年同期少增，主要是居民贷款少增较多，二是新增企业贷款较多，与企
业债发行融资需求偏弱有所不同，三是居民短贷和企业短贷均较去年同期少增较
多。我们认为贷款数据指向了居民部门加杠杆需求不强，以及企业部门内部出现
分化，其中信用等级较高的企业融资需求不强，但依靠信贷的企业中长期贷款需
求仍在。 
 关注外部环境对适度宽松货币政策的影响。9 月底以来，央行货币政策出台了三
项主要调整，一是央行等八部门联合印发《关于金融支持服务业扩能提质的指导
意见》，二是 9 月 29 日发布调整完善若干货币政策工具，包括下调抵押补充贷
款（PSL）利率 0.25 个百分点，将“六张网”建设纳入抵押补充贷款支持领域，及
再贷款扩容等，三是强化财政金融政策协同，实施居民购房贷款贴息政策。整体
来看适度宽松的货币政策仍在发力，但货币政策继续宽松的约束也在加强：从国
内方面看，9 月企业新发放贷款加权平均利率略低于 3%，比上年同期低约 0.2 个
百分点，个人住房新发放贷款加权平均利率约为 3.1%，与上年同期基本持平，9
月底银行间隔夜拆借利率在 1.36%的较低水平，十年期国债利率在 1.68%的较低
水平，人民币对美元汇率增长 10 月初升值突破 6.7；从海外看，市场方面，9 月
底十年美债收益率达到 5.29%的较高水平，同时美联储已于 9 月落地一次加息，
并且纪要显示年内仍将有一次加息。我们认为下一阶段需要关注“适度宽松”的
货币政策可能受外部环境影响，出现国内金融条件“被动收缩”的情况。 
 风险提示：全球通胀二次上行；欧美经济回落速度偏快；国际局势复杂化。

观性的其他职务，没有担任本报告评论的上市公司的董事、监事或高级管理人员；也不拥有与该上市公司有关的任何
财务权益；本报告评论的上市公司或其它第三方都没有或没有承诺向本人提供与本报告有关的任何补偿或其它利益。  
中银国际证券股份有限公司同时声明，将通过公司网站披露本公司授权公众媒体及其他机构刊载或者转发证券研究报
告有关情况 。 如有投资者于未经授权的公众媒体看到或从其他机构获得本研究报告的 ， 请慎重使用所获得的研究报告，
以防止被误导，中银国际证券股份有限公司不对其报告理解和使用承担任何责任。  
评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

2026 年 10 月 14 日 全13 頁

AI の雇用への影響をどう読むか 
一部の職種で狭まる若手の「入口」、経験を積む場をどう残すか

AI アナリティックリサーチ室 主任研究員 新田 尭之

[要約] 
 近年の実証研究では、米国を中心に、生成AI の影響を受けやすい一部の職種で若手の
雇用悪化が相次いで報告されている。悪化は主に採用の減少として表れており、最初
の職を得る「入口」が狭まっている。ただし、悪化の一部は ChatGPT 公開前に始まっ
ており、在宅勤務の広がりやコロナ禍の採用増の反動とも重なる。複数の要因が考え
られる中、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。 
 若手の「入口」が狭まる一方、米国全体では雇用者数が増えている。もっとも、 雇用
増の多くは民間教育・医療によるもので、幅広い産業には広がっていない。採用も離
職も少ないため、既に職に就いている人は職を失いにくいが、これから職を得る人や
転職を望む人にとっては空席が少ない。さらに、AI による省力化は、人員を減らすよ
り、業務の中身や外注を見直す形で進む例が多い。雇用者数だけでは、こうした仕事
の機会の変化は見えにくい。 
 今後の雇用を分けるのは、AI による値下げや品質向上で需要が広がったり、新しい業
務が生まれたりして、どれだけ人手が必要になるかである。ただし、新たな仕事が生
まれても、若手に回るとは限らない。41 カ国の多国籍企業の分析では、生成AI を導入
した企業の拠点で経験者の雇用が増え、若手の比率が下がった。経験者の多くは、入
口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手の採用を絞る判断は、個々の企業には合理的でも、続け
ば将来の経験者が育たない「育成の空洞化」を招きかねない。若手が経験を積む場を
どう残すかが問われており、社内で人を育てる日本企業も無縁ではいられないだろう。

1. はじめに 
近年、生成 AI の進化や普及を背景に、AI が雇用に与える影響について、米国を中心に実証
研究が蓄積されている（図表1）。 
図表 1 生成 AI が雇用に与える影響に関する近年の主な実証研究

（注）各研究の対象・期間・比較方法は異なる。 【 】内は対象の国・地域。「曝露」は仕事の内容が AI で代
替・支援されうる度合い、 「曝露職」はその影響を受けやすい職種を指す。 「高曝露職」は曝露の度合いが高い
職種。 
（出所）各研究より大和総研作成

1

例えば、スタンフォード大学の研究者らは、給与計算・人事サービスを手掛ける ADP の給与
記録を用いて米国の労働市場を分析し、主に次の 6 点を報告している 1。①AI による経済全体
にわたる広範な雇用喪失は確認されない。②一方、AI の影響を受けやすい職種では、受けにく
い職種に比べて22～25 歳の雇用の伸びが弱く、年長層には同程度の差はみられない。③この若
年層の職種間格差は2026 年半ばにかけて拡大を続けた。④その差は主に離職の増加ではなく、
採用の減少による。⑤雇用の減少はAI が人の作業を代替する使い方の多い職種に集中し、仕事
を補完する使い方では雇用の横ばい・増加との関連がみられる。 ⑥調整は基本給よりも雇用の
面に表れている。 
こうした結果は、AI が雇用に与える影響を、全体の人数だけでなく、年齢や採用・離職、AI
の使われ方などに分けて捉える必要性を示している。そこで、本稿ではこうした実証研究や統
計データを基に、①雇用の悪化が目立つ層と、そのうちAI の影響と読める範囲（第 2 章） 、②
雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化（第3 章 ）、 ③今後の雇用を分ける要因（第4 章）
を検討する。

2. 悪化は「入口」で目立つが、AI だけでは説明できない 
（1）AI の影響を受けやすい職種の若年層を中心に悪化 
近年の実証研究を見ると、米国の若年層で雇用の悪化が目立つ。前述のスタンフォード大学
の研究では、22～25 歳の雇用者数は2026 年6 月にChatGPT 公開時の2022 年11 月を約2％下回
った一方、26 歳以上の各年齢層では増加し、特に35～40 歳では約11％多い（図表2）。 
図表 2 年齢層別に見た雇用者数の推移（米国、2022 年 11 月＝1）

（注）ADP の給与記録のうち、2021 年 1 月～2026 年 6 月に継続して観測される企業の 70 歳未満のフルタイム労
働者（毎月 350 万～500 万人） 。年齢層別人口の変化は未調整。 
（出所）Brynjolfsson, Chandar, and Chen [2026]より大和総研作成（画像の加工・仮訳は大和総研）

1 Brynjolfsson, Chandar, and Chen [2026]. AI 曝露はEloundou et al. [2024] の指標のうち GPT-4 による
判定の値で、職業を五つの群に分けて比べている。

ただし、若年層の雇用が職種を問わず悪化したわけではない。同研究の職種別の推計では、
AI への曝露（仕事の内容がAI で代替・支援されうる度合い）が最も高い職種群の22～25 歳の
雇用の伸びは、2022 年11 月から2026 年6 月までに、最も低い職種群の雇用の伸びを約18％ポ
イント下回った。若年層の中でも、AI の影響を受けやすい職種で悪化が目立つ。 
さらに、米国国勢調査局の研究者らは、大学の卒業記録と雇用の行政記録を 接続し、専攻別
に卒業生の就職状況を比べた。これによると、 AI への曝露が最も高い専攻の卒業生は、
ChatGPT 公開直後から、卒業翌四半期に就業している割合が曝露の低い専攻の卒業生と比べて
低下し始め、2024 年以降は約5％ポイント低い2。 
米国の約6,500 万人分の職歴データを企業単位で分析した研究でも、生成AI の導入企業では
若手（職位の低い層）の雇用が2023 年初めから6 四半期で比較対象の企業より約9％減った。
これも主に採用の減少によるという 3。求人の側では、求人サイト Indeed の分析で、テック職
で 5 年以上の経験を求める割合の上昇が確認されている4。 
ニューヨーク連銀が集計したデータからも、若年大卒者の悪化は確認できる。22～27 歳の若
年大卒者の失業率は、2018 年までほぼ一貫して全労働者を下回っていたが、2019 年以降はおお
むね上回るようになり、2026 年 6 月は 5.7％と全労働者（4.1％）を 1.6％ポイント上回る。大
卒でない若年層（22～27 歳）の失業率が 2025 年秋を頂点に下がり始めた後も、若年大卒者の
失業率は高止まりしている（図表3）5。 
図表 3 全労働者と若年層の失業率（米国）

（注）季節調整値の3 か月移動平均。全労働者は16～65 歳、大卒でない若年層は22～27 歳の学士未満、若年大
卒者は 22～27 歳の学士以上で、いずれも在学中の人を除く。 
（出所）ニューヨーク連銀 “The Labor Market for Recent College Graduates”（2026 年8 月6 日）

若年大卒者の失業率を専攻別に見ると、2024 年の失業率はコンピューター工学が 7.8％、コ
ンピューター科学が 7.0％と、全専攻（4.2％）を大きく上回り、73 専攻の上位 5 位以内に入
る。一方、就職後の年収中央値は 9 万ドル、8.7 万ドルと全専攻（5.8 万ドル）を大きく上回
り、大卒資格を要しない職に就く不完全就業率も低い。就業者の年収水準の高さと、失業率の
高さが併存している（図表4）。 
図表 4 専攻別に見た若年大卒者の失業率と年収中央値（米国、2024 年）

なお、オンラインのフリーランス市場でも、AI の影響を受ける分野では、補完的とみなされ
る技能の分野を含め、経験の浅い人向けの案件が、影響を受けない分野に比べて相対的に減っ
たと報告されている 6。年齢と経験の浅さは同じ区分ではないが、若手の 雇用悪化と経験の浅
い人向けの案件の減少は、最初の仕事を得る機会、いわば「入口」が狭まっている点で共通す
る。 
（2）ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化や在宅勤務の広がりなども重なる 
ただし、こうした悪化の原因すべてを生成AI の影響に帰するのは早計であろう。第一に、悪
化の一部は ChatGPT 公開前に始まっていた。例えば、米国のソフトウェア開発職の求人は、
2022 年 2 月をピークに急減し、ChatGPT 公開時には既にピークを3 分の 1 あまり下回っていた
（図表5）。 
ニューヨーク連銀のブログに掲載された求人データの分析でも、AI 曝露の高い職種の求人の
相対的な減少は2022 年より前に始まっており、2022 年以降に傾向の明確な変化は見られない。

6 Teutloff et al. [2025]. 受注・収入の減少はHui, Reshef, and Zhou [2024] も参照。

同じ分析では、高曝露職の中で若手（職位の低い層）向けと経験者向けの求人がほぼ並行して
動いており、求人の減少は入口の仕事に集中していないという 7。 
図表 5 ソフトウェア開発職の求人指数（米国）

（出所）Indeed Hiring Lab より大和総研作成

第二に、在宅勤務の広がりである。ニューヨーク連銀のブログに掲載された分析によると、
米国の在宅勤務が可能な職業では、若手と年長の大卒者の失業率の差が2019 年から 2024 年に
約 1.4％ポイント広がった 8。これは、在宅勤務が難しい職業の同期間の拡大幅（約0.5％ポイ
ント）を大きく上回る。同分析は、在宅勤務では若手への OJT や上司からの助言が難しくなる
ため、企業が経験の浅い人材の採用をためらいやすくなると指摘している。 米英加豪の採用記
録約 2.4 億件の分析でも、在宅勤務のしやすさを同時に考慮すると、AI 曝露と採用者に占める
若手（職位の低い層）の比率との関係は大きく弱まり、多くの場合、統計的に有意 な関係は確
認できなくなった 9。 
米国外の研究も、若手の雇用の減少を AI だけでは説明していない。デンマークでは、AI 曝
露の高いいくつかの職で若手の雇用が減ったものの、研究者らはその減少はAI チャットボット
を導入した企業によるものではないとしている 10。さらに、韓国銀行の研究者の分析によれば、
韓国では 2022 年 6 月から 2026 年 6 月に 15～29 歳の雇用が 28.5 万人減り、その 94％は AI の
影響を受けやすい産業で生じた 11。同研究は、コロナ禍の採用増の反動や経験者中心の採用、
在宅勤務の広がりなども作用した可能性があると指摘している。

7 Audoly, Guerin, and Topa [2026]. 求人情報などを収集・分析するLightcast の米国の求人データを用い、
AI 曝露はAnthropic が作成した、AI の実際の利用状況を反映した作業単位の指標による。若手（職位の低い
層）向け・経験者向けの区分は Lightcast が求人の記載から判定したもの。求人の数の分析であり、実際に誰
が採用されたかは捉えない点で、第 2 章（1）の研究とは対象が異なる。 
8 Emanuel, Harrington, and Pallais [2026]. 大卒者の18～28 歳と29 歳以上の失業率の差の2019 年からの
変化で、数値は同記事のチャートデータによる。 
9 Lambert and Schindler [2026]. 米英加豪の2017～2025 年のデータで、分析対象は、職名と職歴から判定し
た職位の下位 2 区分が採用者に占める比率。 
10 Humlum and Vestergaard [2026]. AI の影響を受けやすい 11 職業の約 2.5 万人への調査を2024 年 12 月まで
の行政記録と接続。新しい業務や時間の使い方は自己申告による。 
11 Oh and Oh [2026]. 雇用は国民年金の加入者数。AI の影響を受けやすい産業は、AI 曝露（Eloundou et al. 
[2024]）で全産業を 4 分位に分けた上位2 分位の産業。

### Page 7

1

7 / 13

そして先に紹介したスタンフォード大学の研究では 、在宅勤務や金利への感応度を調整して
も、22～25 歳の高曝露職と低曝露職の雇用の伸びの差は大きく変わらないが、学歴構成も含め
て調整するとその差は調整前の約 18％ポイントから約 8％ポイントに縮み、統計的に有意な関
係は確認できなくなる12。 
以上のように、一部の職種で若手の雇用が悪化していることは確認できるが、その原因を生
成 AI に求めるには慎重さが必要である。その影響を見極めるには、ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化
と、公開後に加わった可能性のある変化を分けて考える必要がある。また 、足元の弱さをコロ
ナ禍の採用増の反動だけで説明できるとは限らない。複数の要因が考えられる中で、本稿で取
り上げた研究でも、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。

3. 雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化 
仕事を得る機会を考えるには、雇用者数が増えているかどうかに加え、どの分野で増えてい
るのか、新たな採用がどの程度行われているのかを見る必要がある。また、AI による省力化も、
人員数を変えずに業務や外注の見直しとして進む場合がある。以下では、これら三つの側面を
確認する。 
（1）雇用増は民間教育・医療に偏る 
米国では、ChatGPT が公開された2022 年 11 月から2026 年 8 月までに、情報や専門・事業サ
ービスなど、AI の影響を受けやすい職種の多い産業で雇用は減少した。一方、全体の雇用増を
支えたのは民間教育・医療である。同期間の雇用増約 480 万人のうち、約 3 分の 2（約 325 万
人）を占めた。ただし、直近 1 年の全体の雇用増は約 60 万人にとどまり、そのうち約 54 万人
を民間教育・医療が占める（図表6）。 
図表 6 業種別に見た雇用者数の増減（米国）

（注）その他は全体から掲載した業種を差し引いた残差。 
（出所）米労働統計局（BLS）より大和総研作成

12 この研究では、学歴構成は AI が仕事に影響する経路も反映している可能性があるため、その違いを調整し
て差が縮んでも、AI 以外の要因で説明できたとは限らない、としている。

### Page 8

1

8 / 13

民間教育・医療の雇用者数の伸びは、コロナ禍で落ち込んだ反動にもみえる。ただ、コロナ
禍前の2020 年2 月から見ても、雇用増約680 万人の半分（約340 万人）を占めており、雇用増
の最大の担い手であることは変わらない。全体の雇用増が、幅広い産業での雇用拡大を意味す
るわけではない。 
（2）低採用・低離職の下で就職・転職の機会は限られる 
雇用者数の動きと、新たに職を得る機会の違いは、採用と離職からも確認できる。 米国の求
人労働異動調査（JOLTS）によると、2026 年 1～7 月の採用率は平均 3.3％と、コロナ禍前の
2017～19 年（3.8％）を下回り、失業率が 7～8％台だった 2012～13 年並みに低い（図表 7）。
同じ期間の比較では、離職率も3.7％から3.3％に下がり、自己都合退職率（2.2％→2.0％）も
解雇率（1.2％→1.1％）も低下した。採用率の低さは若手だけの現象ではなく、労働市場全体
にみられる。 
図表 7 採用率と離職率（米国）

（出所）BLS より大和総研作成

離職が少なければ、欠員を埋める採用も生まれにくい。就職・転職の機会が限られる中、 先
行きへの不安から今の職にとどまる動きは「ジョブハギング（仕事にしがみつくこと） 」と呼
ばれ、2025 年 8 月ごろから米国の報道で広く取り上げられている13。米国の履歴書作成サイト
が2026 年2 月に行った調査では、自らをジョブハギングの状態にあると回答した就業者が57％
と、半年前の 45％から増えた。その 7 割は、今後半年以内に AI が自分の雇用に影響すること
を懸念していた 14。就職の入口に立つ人にとっては、いわば「空席の少ない市場」である。 
（3）AI による省力化の影響は、人員の削減より仕事の中身の見直しに表れている 
AI による省力化についても、雇用者数だけでは変化を捉えにくい。OECD が日本を含む7 カ国

13 例えば、Fortune “Workers are ‘job hugging’ in a stagnant labor market, but growing resentment 
means they could bail as soon as the next Great Resignation comes”（2025 年8 月 18 日）。 
14 ResumeBuilder.com “6 in 10 Workers Are Clinging To Their Jobs as Job Hugging Soars in 2026”
（2026 年2 月11 日）。米国の就業者2,188 人へのオンライン調査で、ジョブハギングを、会社への愛着では
なく不安から今の職にとどまることとしている。

### Page 9

1

9 / 13

の中小企業に行った調査では、生成 AI を使う企業の 83％が人員の必要数に影響はないと答え
た。必要な人員数の減少（9％）より、従業員の業務負担（33％）や外注への依存（14％）の
減少を挙げる企業が多い 15。社内の人員数が変わらなくても、外部に依頼する仕事は減りうる。 
前出のデンマークの研究では、時間を節約できた利用者の 85％が、その時間を他の業務に充
てると答え、雇用主が特段の施策を講じていない職場でも、利用者の8％が AI の出力の品質確
認など全く新しい業務を担っていた。研究者らは、こうした状況が、業務の見直しなど計測さ
れにくい投資が先行し、生産性の伸びが遅れて表れる「J カーブ」の初期段階と整合的だとし
ている 16。AI による省力化が、直ちに人員や労働時間の削減につながるのではなく、仕事の配
分を変える形で進んでいることがうかがえる。

4. 新たな仕事が生まれても、若手に回るとは限らない 
第 2 章で紹介した研究では、若手の雇用悪化は、主に「解雇」ではなく「採用されない」形
で表れている。この状態が続くかは、AI による省力化で生まれる余力を企業がどの業務に振り
向け、新たな需要がどれだけ人手を必要とするかにかかっている。 
省力化の後の展開は、主に三つの経路として整理できる 17。①人数を変えずに仕事の中身が
変わる。②既存の社員で対応できる範囲が広がり、採用・外注の抑制や人員削減につながる。
③値下げや品質向上による需要の拡大、新しい業務の創出が人手を必要とし、配置転換や採用
につながる。 
AI 導入後の雇用の動きには、企業規模による違いも見られる。デンマークの研究では、AI を
導入した小規模企業で新たな採用が抑えられ、雇用の増えるペースが鈍ったと報告されている。
一方、大規模企業では、同様の雇用の伸びの鈍化は確認されなかった18。 
②と③は、同じ企業の中で同時に進みうる。41 カ国の多国籍企業を対象とした研究は、AI導
入企業で増えた雇用がどの層に向かったかを示している。生成AI を導入した企業の拠点では、
ChatGPT 公開直後から若手（職位の低い層）の比率が下がり始め、2026 年 3 月には導入してい
ない企業の拠点での比率を 1.9％ポイント下回った（図表 8）19。この比率低下の主因は、若手

の雇用減少ではなく、ChatGPT 公開から約 1 年半後に始まった経験者の雇用増加である。この
研究の著者らは、AI の影響を受けやすい職種では、AI が若手の仕事を省く一方で経験者への需
要を広げたと解釈している。需要が広がっても、その担い手が若手になるとは限らないことを
示す結果である。

③の経路を示す研究もある。米国の上場企業では、AI 投資を増やした企業ほど、主に製品の
革新を通じて売上と雇用が伸びたとされる 20。デンマークの研究で報告された、AI の出力の品
質確認などの新しい業務も③の芽といえる。 
採用の構成も、AI の使い方によって変わりうる。AI を使う既存社員が若手の入口の仕事を担
えば、採用は経験者に寄りうる。逆に、AI の支援で経験の浅い人に任せられる仕事が増えれば、

20 Babina et al. [2024]. 2010～2018 年の非テック上場企業の分析。効果は規模の大きい企業に集中する。他
社からの需要の移転も含みうるが、AI 投資の多い産業では産業全体でも売上と雇用が増えたとされる。

### Page 11

1

11 / 13

若手を採る利点は高まりうる。AI が経験の差を縮めるこうした作用は、「専門性の平準化」と
呼ばれる 21。 
もっとも、これまでの実証研究では、採用が経験者に寄る動きが目立つ。この 動きが続けば、
懸念されるのは「育成の空洞化」である。 経験者の多くは、入口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手採
用の絞り込みは、個々の企業には合理的でも、経済全体では将来の経験者層 を薄くしかねない。
AI の支援と経験者の指導を組み合わせ、若手が経験を積む場を残す工夫が求められる。 
日本企業もこの問題と無縁ではいられないだろう。日本では少子高齢化で人手不足が続き、
新卒を一括採用して社内で育てる企業が多い。このため、影響は採用数より、採る職種の構成
や、人手の足りない部署への配置転換に先に表れる可能性がある 22。とはいえ、採用数が保た
れても、AI が入口の仕事を担えば、若手が経験を積む場は細りうる。問われているのは「AI で
若手の仕事をどこまで省けるか」ではなく、 「AIを使いながら若手をどう育てるか」である。

2 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

4 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has

5 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

Investing involves risk, including the possible loss of principal. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.

This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and
investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.

of Bank of America Corporation.

of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

liquidity tensions going forward.

Vietnam’s export base has strengthened further over the past year, notwithstanding the risin
 g 
US tariffs
 , notably 
because 
 Vietnam 
 has been a  
key beneficiar
 y 
of the surgin
 g 
global demand for AI
 -
 related goods  
◼ 
 Export growth strengthened in 2025 (+17% in value), driven by electronic goods (+48%) and sales to the U
 nited 
S
tates 
(+28%). V
ietnam 
was 
 one of 
the main targets of President Trump’s tariff war in early 2025, but the tariff shock was much smaller than initially feared, 
and exports were boosted by surgin
 g 
AI
-
 related demand. V
 ietnam  
increased its global market share (1.9% of world exports 
 in 2025 
vs. 1.7% in 2024), supported by gains in the US market (5.7% of US imports vs. 4.2% in 2024) and the semiconductor sector (
 Vietnam 
is estimated  
to 
account for  
2.9% of world exports of semiconductors vs. 1.6% in 2024).  
◼ 
 Solid growth dynamics in industrial 
 out
p
ut 
and exports ha
 ve 
continued 
 in 2026
 . Exports rose by 
 21.9% y/y in the first eight months 
of the year
 , 
led by the strong increase in both volume and price of tech goods.  
However, 
 Vietnam  
adds little value to the tech goods  
that 
it exports as it speciali
 s
es mostly in assembl
 ing 
and packaging chips to form electronic components. 
 Therefore, i
 ts balance of 
trade of semiconductor goods is negative
 , as i
 mports and exports of tech goods are increasing in tandem.  
◼ 
 V

 ’s trade surplus with the U
 nited 
 S

has

 recently

It

USD213 bn

◼ 
 Meanwhile, V
 ietnam
 ’s imports of Chinese goods have increased rapidly (+3

% y/y in 
 the first eight months of 
 2026), causing its 
trade deficit with China to surge.  
Indeed, V
 ietnam  
has played a growing role in the rerouting of Chinese goods to the US. It has also 
benefited largely from the reorgani
 s
ation of production chains in the region and continued to attract

Asian countries. Net FDI inflows have exceeded 4% of GDP in recent years, which has fostered continued expansion of the 
manufacturing sector and progress up the value chain.  
◼ 
 In the

 ,

 that

 that

◼ 
 US trade policy remains 
 a source of 
 uncertain
 ty
. The US effective tariff rate on V

to 19.3% in Aug

 has fallen

 in 2026 (
 as the US Supreme Court cancelled ‘reciprocal’ tariffs)
 ; it is now close to 10
 %. This 
is higher than

 , 
 but not high enough to undermine the competitiveness of 
 Vietnamese 
 goods 
substantially
 . However, Vietnam is

 and 
 the U

S

 S
tates
.

Vietnam  
is a net energy importer highly exposed to the consequences of the conflict in the Middle East. So far, 
the impact of hydrocarbon supply disruptions has been 
 more moderate  
than expected, but it could 
 worsen 
 in 
the coming months.  
◼ 
 Vietnam 
 is vulnerable to disruptions in energy trade flows
 . I
n 2025, 
 88% of its total crude oil imports came from Kuwait and 49% of 
its gas imports  
were 
from the Gulf region. However, a few factors have attenuated the shock  
resulting from the Iran war
 , as  
V
ietnam  
has reacted swiftly, finding alternative suppliers, introducing measures to contain demand and using its (low) stocks. 
 The country 
produces 70% of its needs for refined products and imports the remainder from Asian countries  
(
though the region’s production is 
dependent on imports from the Gulf countries
 )
. More positively, oil 
 and

 Vietnam
 ’s energy mix (coal: 54% 
and 
renewables: 11%), and the country can part
 ial
 ly switch to coal for electricity generation.  
◼ 
 The potential pass
 -
 through of rising global energy prices to domestic inflation is high. Yet, the government has some fiscal policy 
leeway 
 (

 )

4.

,

 , unless inflation accelerates more than expected or depreciation pressures on the dong intensify too much.

VIETNAM HAS NOT YET FIXED ITS EXTERNAL LIQUIDITY PROBLEM
, WHICH IS A SOURCE OF VULNERABILITY 
◼ 
 V
ietnam
 ’s trade balance 
 has 
 deteriorated 
 steadily 
 in 2026. It turned in
 to a deficit  
amounting to 
 USD
19 
bn 
in the first eight months of 
2026 
 (customs data)
 , 
 compared to  
 a surplus 
 in the previous year 
 (
amounting to 
 USD
14 
 bn 
 in the first eight months of 2025
 ).  
This 
 rapid deterioration 
 has resulted notably from the ris
 ing 
import prices of both energy and electronic inputs.  
◼ 
 On the one hand, the current account surplus 
 is 
 decreas
 ing, bu
 t from a comfortable level
 . 
It is projected to narrow 
 to 
2.9
% of GDP in 
2026 from 6.7% in 2025, due to  
the larger energy trade deficit (
 which 
 accounted for  
3.6% of GDP in 2025), the larger trade deficit in 
semiconductors/tech goods, and lower export receipts from tourism 
 (about 
 6% of GDP). 
 Furthermore
 , net FDI  
 inflows will remain 
strong,  
staying close to 4% of GDP in 2026
 . 
Therefore, 
 Vietnam is  
unlikely to need to incur new foreign debt.  
◼ 
 On the other hand, V
 ietnam
 ’s forex reserves are low and do not provide a solid cushion  
 against a deterioration 
 in 
 the balance of 
payments
 . Forex reserves amounted to USD8
 8
.
3 
bn at the end of 
 June 2026
 , which is 
 weak: it is 
 enough 
to service external debt in 
the 
 short ter
 m 
 but covers less than 3 months of imports of goods 
 and 
 services. In 
 particular, i
 f 
 resident capital outflows increase 
(residents’ confidence in the dong 
 may 
be eroded by ri
 s
in
g 
inflation and the external energy shock), currency pressure could intensify 
and the central bank could have difficulties to defend the dong. 
 This risk cannot be ignored
 , 
 even 
 though 
 t
he VND has barely 
depreciated 
 this year (
 the

the beginning of 2026
 )

MEDIUM
-
TERM PROSPECTS ARE FAVO
U
RABLE 
◼ 
 In the medium term, economic growth is expected to remain strong. It is projected at 5.9% per year on average in 2027
 –
2031, 
compared to  
6.2% in the past decade. This is one of the highest growth rates expected in the ASEAN region.  
◼ 
 This  
projection is based on a rather optimistic scenario, as it assumes that:  
i
) FDI continues to flow in, and the manufacturing export sector expands further
 . This 
 means Vietnam’s external accounts 
 continue 
to 
strengthen in the medium term, thanks to continued 
 current account surpluses
 , 
lower resident capital outflows, declining external 
debt and slowly increasing forex reserves
 ; 
ii
) 
D
emographic trends remain 
 favourable
 , even 
 though they 
 are projected to 
 weaken gradually  
in the medium
 -
 long term
 ;  
iii
) 
 Structural r
 eforms make steady progress, helping to improve financial
 -
sector supervision, ease infrastructure constraints in the 
energy and water sectors, boost aggregate productivity (
 i.e. 
 reducing the gap between high
 -
 productivity FDI firms and low
 -
productivity local sectors) and adapt to climate change.

COUNTRY RISK PROFILE
: STRONG ECONOMIC GROWTH AND LOW PUBLIC 
AND 
FOREIGN DEBT VS. LOW EXTERNAL 
LIQUIDITY AND 
A 
WEAK BANKING SECTOR

◼ 
 Strong factors support 
 Vietnam’s 
 overall country risk  
and economic prospects
 :  
i) 
a 
strong manufacturing export base that is moving up the value chain
 ; 
ii) 
s
olid comparative advantages that continue to attract large FDI inflows
 ; 
iii) 
c
urrent account surpluses and moderate foreign debt
 ; 
iv) 
a 
moderate government debt burden
 ; 
v) 
g
radually improving policy making
 ; 
vi) 
 s
trong medium
 -
 term economic growth prospects, assuming  
 that 
 the authorities will continue to implement structural reforms 
and strengthen productivity in the entire economy.

◼ 
 On the negative side,  
major negative factors continue to fuel some macro
 -
 financial risks:  
i) t
 he excessive domestic debt level of the economy (excluding the government) and fragilities in the banking sector 
 – 
this is a bad 
combination that fuels high credit risks and financial instability risks
 ; 
ii) 
 low forex reserves
 , which 
 have not much strengthened in recent years
 , 
 despite current account surpluses and notably due to 
resident capital flight. Forex reserves do not  
provide good  
protect
ion 
against shocks, while 
 Vietnam 
 is vulnerable to external shocks
 ;  
iii) 
 v
ery high exposure to physical climate risk.  
 C
oastal and river
 -
 delta regions, where population and economic activity are 
concentrated, 
 are 
 highly exposed to sea
 -
 level rise
 s 
and flooding
 .

◼ 
 Non transfer risk and currency risk
 : 
 V
ietnam  
 has enjoyed very 
 favourable  
 external account dynamics in recent years  
 until 2025
 , 
thanks to 
 large FDI inflows
 , 
recurrent 
 current
 -
 account surpluses  
and declining f
 oreign debt (
 which is moderate, at 
 less than  
30% of 
both GDP and exports). 
 This trend 
 has been  
interrupted 
 in 
 2026 
as 
current account surpluses 
 are 
 decreasing
 . Meanwhile
 , 
there is a 
persist
 ent 
 risk of 
 increasing 
 resident capital outflows 
 fuelling  
 some 
 VND 
 depreciation pressure  
 (
w
hereas  
 the potential for foreign 
capital outflows is limited
 )
. 
As 
the forex  
reserve cushion is weak
 , it would 
 not enable the 
 central bank 
 to 
defend the dong for a long 
time 
i
f there is  
severe currency and external liquidity pressure
 . 
However, so far this year, 
 the 
VND/USD spot rate and the reference 
rate 
 (Vietnam has a crawling band FX regime) 
 have depreciated only slightly
 , supported by strong exports and FDI inflows
 .

◼ 
 Sovereign risk
 : 
the government debt burden 
 is moderate and 
 will remain 
 so in the short term, even if the fiscal deficit is projected to 
deteriorate
 . The fiscal deficit could exceed 3% of GDP in 2026 due to support measures in response to the energy shock (estimated at 
less than 1% of GDP in H1 2026), but this will not endanger public
 -
 debt dynamics. Government debt should fall again in 2027 thanks 
to lower deficits and fast GDP growth. Debt was only 30% of GDP in 2025 (vs. 41% in 2019) and interest payments consumed less  
than 5% of fiscal revenue (vs. 7.2% in 2019). The government cover
 s its funding needs mostly in the local bond market, which is 
deepening gradually and not 
 highly 
 exposed to foreign investors. Government debt in foreign currency declined to about 25% of total 
debt in 2025 (vs. 38% in 2019); this is still substantial, but almost all this FC debt 
 is made up  
 of concessional loans from official 
creditors. On the negative front, high credit risks in the state corporate and bank sectors 
 are 
 very large contingent risks for the 
sovereign. Moreover, public investment needs related to climate change and infrastructure development will increase in the 
 medium 
term
.

◼ 
 Systemic credit risk
 : 
Domestic credit to the private sector is too large and still rising (141% of GDP in 2025 vs. 108% in 2019) while 
the banking sector remains poorly capitali
 s
ed. Credit risks and resource misallocation have long been exacerbated by opacity and 
supervision flaws, as well as the use of high credit growth targets to support economic growth (+15% in 2026). In 2022
 –
2023, banks 
faced severe funding stress and asset
 -
 quality deterioration resulting from the property and bond market crisis. The situation has 
recovered gradually since then. In the 
 short term
 , credit risks may increase amid higher energy costs and if  
there is  
a correction in 
the tech cycle. Some banks, especially small private ones, also remain exposed to VND funding risk (whereas banks do not rely  
much 
on 
 foreign
-
 currency 
 financing). Positively, reforms, including stricter capital
 -
 adequacy requirements, continue to be introduced 
gradually 
 in order 
 to improve banks’ creditworthiness (
 the 
 SBV plans to test the removal of credit quotas for the soundest banks 
from 2026).

Tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 được dự 
báo khả quan so với cùng kỳ

NIM chịu áp lực trước mục tiêu tăng 
trưởng

Chất lượng tài sản suy giảm nhẹ do 
mặt bằng lãi suất ở mức cao

Khuyến nghị: MUA – Giá mục tiêu 
25,950 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 đạt 27%, với động lực chính 
đến từ phân khúc KHDN, đặc biệt là từ nhóm FDI trong bối cảnh dòng vốn FDI vào 
Việt Nam và hoạt động xuất nhập khẩu duy trì tích cực. Xét về lĩnh vực, cho vay kinh 
doanh BĐS kỳ vọng sẽ tăng trưởng vượt trội khi ngân hàng tham gia vào thu xếp 
vốn cho các dự án hạ tầng và dự án BĐS ưu tiên, trong khi các mảng kinh doanh 
khác tăng trưởng ở mức khả quan.

Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng NIM giảm so với năm 2025 do áp lực huy động khiến chi 
phí vốn duy trì ở mặt bằng cao, trong khi lãi suất cho vay dự kiến ở mức cạnh tranh 
để ưu tiên mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín dụng.

Chúng tôi dự phóng tỷ lệ nợ xấu của ngân hàng sẽ tăng 11bps YoY trong bối cảnh lãi 
suất đang neo ở mức cao và kỳ vọng ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì chính sách trích 
lập dự phòng thận trọng nhằm củng cố bộ đệm, qua đó hoàn thành mục tiêu duy 
trì tỷ lệ bao phủ nợ xấu ở mức trên 100%.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh của ngân hàng cũng như đánh giá các yếu tố rủi ro, 
chúng tôi tiếp tục duy trì khuyến nghị MUA với cổ phiếu MBB với giá mục tiêu 
25,950 VNĐ/CP - tiềm năng tăng giá 26.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 17/09/2026.

Cơ cấu tín dụng  Hoạt động kinh doanh

Ngân hàng TMCP Quân đội (MBB) được 
thành lập năm 1994 với định hướng hoạt 
động ban đầu là cung cấp các dịch vụ tài 
chính cho các doanh nghiệp quân đội. Sau 
hơn 30 năm phát triển, MBB hiện đã trở 
thành ngân hàng sở hữu quy mô tài sản và 
tín dụng lớn nhất trong nhóm NH TMCP tư 
nhân. Phát triển theo mô hình tập đoàn, 
MBB đi đầu trong lĩnh vực chuyển đổi số với 
số lượng khách hàng và tỷ lệ CASA đầu 
ngành. 
Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

mức vượt trội so với ngành. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Kết quả kinh doanh tích cực nhờ 
tăng trưởng thu nhập lãi thuần 
 Tín dụng trong 2Q2026 tăng mạnh so với quý trước, đạt 13.2% YTD cùng sự 
phục hồi của NIM đã giúp ngân hàng thiết lập kỷ lục mới về thu nhập lãi thuần 
với 16,894 tỷ đồng (+36.5% YoY). Thu nhập ngoài lãi tiếp tục thu hẹp trong quý 
này do hoạt động kinh doanh ngoại hối và chứng khoán kém tích cực, qua đó 
phần nào làm giảm tốc tăng trưởng tổng thu nhập hoạt động (+18.5% YoY). Chi 
phí dự phòng rủi ro đạt 4,247 tỷ đồng (-11.3% YoY), qua đó giúp LNTT tăng tích 
cực (+40.7% YoY), đạt 10,560 tỷ đồng. Lũy kế 1H2026, LNTT của ngân hàng đạt 
20,188 tỷ đồng, hoàn thành 51% kế hoạch BLĐ đề ra đầu năm.

Bảng 1. Cập nhật Kết quả kinh doanh 
(tỷ VNĐ) Q2/2025 Q1/2026 Q2/2026 +/-%YoY +/-QoQ Chú thích 
Thu nhập lãi thuần  12,372   14,913   16,894  36.5% 13.3% Tăng trưởng tín dụng ở mức cao so với cùng kỳ cùng sự phục 
hồi của NIM trong quý 2 đã hỗ trợ thu nhập lãi thuần tiếp tục

### Page 5

5

Tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 được dự 
báo khả quan so với cùng kỳ  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự báo tăng trưởng tín dụng cả năm 2026 ở mức 27%, vượt 
trội hơn mặt bằng chung của ngành nhờ lợi thế tham gia nhận CGBB. Cụ thể: 
− Xét về phân khúc khách hàng, động lực chính kỳ vọng sẽ đến từ phân 
khúc KHDN, trong đó nổi bật là nhóm FDI khi ngân hàng đã chủ động 
thành lập khối Khách hàng FDI chuyên biệt trong bối cảnh dòng vốn 
FDI vào Việt Nam và hoạt động xuất nhập khẩu duy trì tích cực.  
− Xét về lĩnh vực, chúng tôi cho rằng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS sẽ tăng 
trưởng vượt trội với kỳ vọng ngân hàng tham gia vào thu xếp các dự án 
hạ tầng và dự án BĐS ưu tiên (BĐS KCN, NOXH). Ngân hàng cho biết tỷ 
trọng dư nợ mảng này dự kiến sẽ được duy trì trong biên độ mục tiêu 
13±2% tổng dư nợ tín dụng. Chúng tôi nghiêng về khả năng ngân hàng 
sẽ bám sát cận trên của mục tiêu (15%), nhằm tận dụng dư địa tăng 
trưởng từ các dự án ưu tiên, đồng thời duy trì kiểm soát chất lượng tài 
sản an toàn. Trong khi đó, dư nợ cho vay các mảng khác dự kiến sẽ ở 
mức thấp hơn tăng trưởng chung do môi trường vĩ mô còn nhiều khó 
khăn.

Biểu đồ 8. Cơ cấu tín dụng 
Tỷ trọng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS liên tục gia tăng 
trong các quý gần đây, trong khi cơ cấu cho vay 
thương mại và chế biến chế tạo có xu hướng giảm. 
Tính tới hết 1H2026, tăng trưởng cho vay kinh doanh 
BĐS, thương mại, và chế biến chế tạo lần lượt đạt 
38.2%, 4.9%, và 9.3% YTD.

Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

NIM chịu áp lực giảm so với 2025  Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng NIM cả năm sẽ giảm so với 2025 (-15bps YoY) dựa 
trên luận điểm:  
(1) Ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục dịch chuyển cơ cấu huy động sang các kênh có 
chi phí vốn (COF) cao hơn nhằm đáp ứng mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín 
dụng cao, trong bối cảnh cạnh tranh huy động trên thị trường 1 vẫn 
diễn ra gay gắt. Bên cạnh đó, ngân hàng cho biết dự kiến sẽ phát hành 
10-15 nghìn tỷ trái phiếu tier 2 trong 2H2026 với COF tương đối cao. 
(2) Với mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín dụng cao, chúng tôi cho rằng ngân hàng 
sẽ duy trì lãi suất cho vay ở mức cạnh tranh so với mặt bằng chung của 
ngành. Các khoản vay dự kiến vẫn sẽ tiếp tục tái định giá theo mặt 
bằng lãi suất hiện tại, tuy nhiên tốc độ sẽ chậm lại so với quý 2, do đó 
lợi suất IEA dự kiến sẽ tăng chậm hơn COF trong 2H2026.

Chất lượng tài sản suy giảm trong 
tầm kiểm soát  
 Trong 2Q2026, tỷ lệ hình thành nợ xấu ở mức 1.2% (-16bps QoQ), tương ứng 
4,142 tỷ đồng nợ xấu phát sinh mới, và tỷ lệ nợ xấu tăng nhẹ 3bps so với quý 
trước. Nợ xấu tăng trong quý này chủ yếu tới từ phân khúc bán lẻ (do hoạt động 
kinh doanh và giá đất của khách hàng tại địa phương bị ảnh hưởng sau khi sáp

Cho cả năm 2026, chúng tôi dự phóng tỷ lệ nợ xấu của ngân hàng sẽ tăng 11bps 
YoY trong bối cảnh lãi suất đang neo ở mức cao. Cùng với đó, chúng tôi kỳ vọng 
ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì chính sách trích lập dự phòng thận trọng nhằm 
củng cố bộ đệm, qua đó hoàn thành mục tiêu duy trì tỷ lệ bao phủ nợ xấu ở mức 
trên 100%.
